Candide (First English Translation, 1760) (Voltaire, 1760)
==========================================================
Anonymous first English translation ('Candidus; or, All for the Best,' London: M. Cooper, 1760), predating Smollett's; includes the spurious contemporary Part II as published. Public domain. Sourced from Internet Archive item bim_eighteenth-century_candide-english-cand_voltaire_1760 (https://archive.org/details/bim_eighteenth-century_candide-english-cand_voltaire_1760).
